### English

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:3`

## RED_EYE_BEAM_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ARED_EYE_BEAM_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

一种生物的晶状体被增强以发射高强度红色激光束。对基因型进行进一步改造可以触发多个眼球的同步发育。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:3`

### English

The lens of a creature's eyes are enhanced to project high-intensity red laser beams. Further modification of the genome triggers the spontaneous growth of additional eyes.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:4`

## BLUE_EYE_BEAM_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABLUE_EYE_BEAM_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

通过对视网膜进行基因改良可以让眼部器官投射出更强大的蓝色能量束。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:9`

### English

Genetically refining the retina allows ocular organs to project a more powerful blue energy beam.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:11`

## UV_EYE_BEAM_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUV_EYE_BEAM_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

紫外投射器覆盖在视网膜上，以释放更精确和破坏性的激光束。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:15`

### English

UV projectors shield the retina in order to discharge a more accurate and destructive laser beam.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:18`

### English

## BIO_PLASMA_THROWER_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_PLASMA_THROWER_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这种基因修饰刺激了能产生等离子体的腺体和桶状突起的生长，可以将等离子体投射到很远的地方。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:33`

### English

This genome modification stimulates the growth of plasma-generating glands and barrel-like protuberances to project this plasma over long distances.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:39`

## BIO_PLASMA_ACCELERATOR_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_PLASMA_ACCELERATOR_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

改良有机加速器能够提高射程，避免等离子体外泄，使其能发射更快且更具破坏力的抛射体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:39`

### English

Refining the organic accelerators increases range and prevents plasma leakage, creating a faster and more destructive projectile.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:46`

该突变让生物可以制造高浓缩的质子作为强力的抛射弹丸。然而，充能过程可能会较为漫长。

### 中文

这种生物武器可以绕过常规防御直接攻击目标本身的组成原子，将化学键敲碎造成致命的船体伤害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:87`

### English

## MEGA_BOMBARD

### English

Mega Bombard

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:234`

## MEGA_BOMBARD_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMEGA_BOMBARD_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这种轰炸性质的变异笨重且具有侵入性，严重阻碍了生物的行动，需要精准的瞄准才能发挥作用。然而，这些缺点被武器的强大破坏力所弥补。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:201`

### English

Cumbersome and invasive, this bombardment mutation heavily impedes a creature's movements and requires precise targeting to be effective. These drawbacks are more than compensated for by the weapon's raw destructive power.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:235`

### 中文

有机膛线的改进使这种轰炸武器能够以更高的速度发射更大的弹丸，从而实现极其强大的炮击。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:207`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMIASMA_LAUNCHER&lang=en)

### 中文

瘴气发射器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:212`

### English

Miasma Launcher

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:248`

## MIASMA_LAUNCHER_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMIASMA_LAUNCHER_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

该生物武器由一个孕育舱和一个半成形的易爆幼体组成，有效地充当了定向导弹。一旦与孕育舱分离，爆炸性幼体将本能地向目标发射并在撞击时爆炸。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:213`

### English

Composed of a gestative pod and a partially-formed volatile spawn, this bio-weapon effectively functions as a targeted missile. Once separated from its pod, the explosive spawn will instinctively launch itself toward the chosen target and detonate on impact.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:249`

### 中文

通过改变生成弹药的形成过程，将不稳定反物质有效载荷整合到其中，可以提高爆炸威力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:225`

## QUANTUM_LAUNCHER_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AQUANTUM_LAUNCHER_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

孕育过程的改进使得量子产卵弹的制造成为可能。这些活体导弹可以从零点真空汲取能量，释放出难以置信的破坏力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:231`

### English

Improvements in the gestation process allows for the creation of quantum spawn projectiles. These living missiles can draw energy from a zero point vacuum to unleash incredible destructive power.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:270`

## MARAUDER_LAUNCHER_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMARAUDER_LAUNCHER_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

基因改造赋予了该投射生物近乎完整的意识，提升了飞行性能和弱点瞄准能力。这种增强带来的道德影响相当复杂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:237`

### English

Genetic tweaking has granted the projectile creature near-complete sentience, improving flight performance and weak-spot targeting. The moral implications of this enhancement are somewhat complex.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:277`

### 中文

### English

Spore Launcher

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:283`

## REGULAR_SPORE_LAUNCHER_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AREGULAR_SPORE_LAUNCHER_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

一种瘴气发射器的变体，它用一团孢子球代替了半智能弹丸，这些孢子球会在撞击时爆炸。弹丸的高密度使得这种变异很难被点防御系统拦截。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:243`

### English

A variant of the miasma launcher that replaces its semi-sentient projectile with a cloud of spore bulbs that explode on impact. The high density of projectiles makes this mutation difficult to intercept with point-defense systems.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:284`

### English

## BIO_TORPEDOES_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_TORPEDOES_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

由多余覆层形成的生物鱼雷，内部充满爆炸性液体。特殊的等离子涂层使这些制导弹药能够穿透能量屏障。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:255`

### English

Bio-torpedoes formed from excess mantle and filled with explosive fluids. A special plasmic coating allows these guided munitions to pass through energy barriers.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:298`

## CARAPACE_BIO_TORPEDOES

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ACARAPACE_BIO_TORPEDOES&lang=en)

### 中文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:260`

### English

Carapace Bio-Torpedoes

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:304`

## CARAPACE_BIO_TORPEDOES_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ACARAPACE_BIO_TORPEDOES_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

生物鱼雷添加了装甲外壳，使其能够抵御点防御弹药。爆炸后，外壳会碎裂成穿透性的弹片，从而提高破坏力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:261`

### English

The addition of an armored carapace allows bio-torpedoes to withstand point-defense munitions. Once detonated, this carapace shatters into piercing shrapnel, increasing destructive power.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:305`

### 中文

提高爆炸载荷的浓度和粘度，使其在爆炸前更好地穿透目标的防御。

## LARGE_SCOURGE_MISSILE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALARGE_SCOURGE_MISSILE&lang=en)

### 中文

虫灾导弹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:272`

### English

Scourge Missile

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:318`

## LARGE_SCOURGE_MISSILE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALARGE_SCOURGE_MISSILE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这些装甲弹药整合了虫群天灾的遗传物质，它们会将受害者分解，以便之后吞噬。它们在远程攻击中致命，并且具有很高的耐久性，结合了导弹和鱼雷的最强特性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:273`

### English

Integrating the genetic material of the Prethoryn Scourge, these armored munitions break apart their victims to facilitate later consumption. Deadly at long range and individually durable, they combine the strongest traits of both missiles and torpedoes.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:319`

## BARB_BATTERIES

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABARB_BATTERIES&lang=en)

### 中文

针刺炮组

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:278`

### English

Barb Batteries

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:325`

## BARB_BATTERIES_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABARB_BATTERIES_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

生物大炮构成的炮组可发射集束爆炸针刺。这种武器专门用于控制大片空间以拦截敌方舰载机和导弹。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:279`

### English

Bio-gun batteries that discharge clusters of explosive barbs. This weapon specializes in controlling large swathes of space to intercept enemy strike craft and missiles.

## NANITE_INFUSED_BARB_ARTILLERY_DESC

爆炸针刺能够在引爆时产生纳米云。这些微型促动器能够以极快的速度吞噬敌方的舰载机。

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:347`

这个器官可以以极快的速度“啐射”少量腐蚀性物质。额外的眼部受体可以精确追踪来袭的攻击机和导弹。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:303`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:354`

## SUPER_FLAGELLA_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASUPER_FLAGELLA_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

对鞭毛的组织进行基因改造，创造了一种超越自然界任何生物的变形虫亚种。这种被称为“超级鞭毛”的半自主舰载机拥有强大的攻击能力，同时对弹药的抵抗力也更强。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:339`

### English

A genetic overhaul of the flagella organism has created an Amoeba subspecies that surpasses anything found in nature. Known as 'Super Flagella', these semi-autonomous strike craft offer immense offensive capabilities as well as increased resistance to munitions.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:396`

## ANCIENT_COLLAPSER_BEAM_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AANCIENT_COLLAPSER_BEAM_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

一种基于远古蓝图的自调控激光透镜。这种眼光线在强度和频率上产生自发波动以凿穿装甲板，是一种致命高效的武器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:363`

### English

A self-regulating laser lens based on ancient blueprints. This eye beam fluctuates automatically in intensity and frequency to carve through armor plating, functioning as a deadly and efficient weapon.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:424`

## ANCIENT_MACRO_SHARD_BATTERIES_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AANCIENT_MACRO_SHARD_BATTERIES_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这种有机武器利用空间中存在的电场来过载大量的硬化骨片。是生物线圈武器的一种衍生版本，这种散射弹药精准度较低，但可以从更远的距离攻击目标。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:369`

### English

This organic weapon harnesses electric fields present in space to supercharge a large quantity of hardened bone shards. A variant on bio-coil weaponry, this spread munitions are less accurate but can engage targets from longer range.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:431`

## ANCIENT_NANO_TALON_CLOUD_LAUNCHER_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AANCIENT_NANO_TALON_CLOUD_LAUNCHER_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

仿照一份远古设计图，这种类似雄蕊的器官会发射出大量的微型生物鱼雷孢子。其较小的口径能让弹药直接切断外部防御和破坏关键的内部系统。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:381`

### English

Following an ancient schematic, this organ resembling a flower's stamen launches volleys of mini bio-torpedo spores. The smaller caliber allows for the munitions to cut through external defenses and damage critical internal systems directly.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:445`

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:508`

## NEUTRONIUM_CARAPACE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ANEUTRONIUM_CARAPACE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

刺激生长零素装甲板需要将生物的基因组推至极限。这种卓越的坚固甲壳可以抵御能瞬间液化其他防御的伤害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:471`

### English

Stimulating the growth of Neutronium armor plates requires pushing the creature's genome to its limits. This extraordinarily robust carapace can withstand damage that would liquify other defenses.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:550`

## DRACONIC_CARAPACE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADRACONIC_CARAPACE&lang=en)

### 中文

龙鳞甲壳

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:476`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:556`

## DRACONIC_CARAPACE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADRACONIC_CARAPACE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

通过对太空巨龙基因的分析，我们获得了将其遗传物质与我们生物的遗传物质拼接起来的必要工具。这种融合将使它们的甲壳上长出坚固到难以置信的龙鳞，能大大提高生存能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:477`

### English

Through the analysis of the space dragon's genome, we've gained the essential tools to splice its genetic material with our creatures'. This fusion would enable the growth of impossibly strong dragon scales atop their carapaces, significantly enhancing their survivability.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:557`

### English

## ANCIENT_ENERGISED_CARAPACE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AANCIENT_ENERGISED_CARAPACE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这种充能甲壳是根据古代盔甲板的图案设计的。超强能量编织在生物再生表皮和坚韧皮革层之间，既可以充当护盾，也可以充当盔甲。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:501`

### English

This energized carapace was designed following ancient patterns of armor plating. Supercharged energy is woven between layers of bio-generating epidermis and durable hide, able to serve as both shields and armor.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:585`

## MANEUVERING_TENTACLES_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMANEUVERING_TENTACLES_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

$CAMOUFLAGE_1_TOOLTIP$\n\n§E如果仅装备有$PSIONIC_SKIN$或者$PSIONIC_EPIDERMIS$则可以避免$SHIP_SHIELD_REDUCTION_CLOAKING$。装有任何其他类型的护盾都会失去该效果。§!\n\n

## VIRULENT_CYSTS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AVIRULENT_CYSTS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

充满繁殖液的囊肿使每个处于繁殖的三头虫形态的虚空虫在轰炸有人居住的星球时都能独立产卵。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:632`

### English

Cysts filled with reproductive fluid allow for each Voidworm in a fertile Troika to independently produce spawn when bombarding an inhabited planet.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:738`

## FISSILE_CORES_DESC

### 中文

冗余的思维中心允许晶态实体将自身一分为二，作为最后的巧谋来保护它的后代免受特别危险的敌人的攻击。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_mutations_l_simp_chinese.yml:653`

### English

Redundant thought centers allow for a Crystalline Entity to split itself in two as a final gambit to protect its clutch against a particularly dangerous foe.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_mutations_l_english.yml:763`
